# Conformance report

Date: 2026-11-14. Audited commit: `5e03984ad459c8ed30d11896117b7cd6ebc44752` (all line numbers refer to it). Documents: 117.

Code-only audit (no compile or live testing). Each document was walked section by section (spec-down) and each source file behaviour by behaviour (code-up); rows found by only one sweep were re-audited, then every fragment was verified. Only non-conformant requirements are listed below; per-section coverage ledgers stay in `target/conformance/`.

## RFC 1034: Domain names - concepts and facilities

| Section | Code location | Explanation | Documented |
|---|---|---|---|
| §2.2 AXFR Response | `crates/pharos-server/src/transfer.rs:195-217`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/secondary/update.rs:87-130` | **Violation**: The AXFR client accepts any final SOA and does not compare it with the opening SOA. It can publish a transfer whose closing SOA differs from the opening SOA. | No |
| §2.2 AXFR Response | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/secondary/update.rs:92-127`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:135-198` | **Violation**: The AXFR client appends every received RR, and Zone construction retains duplicates. RFC 5936 requires clients to ignore duplicate RRs. | No |
| §3 Negative Answers from Authoritative Servers | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:827-853`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:445-457` | **Violation**: Negative answers include the zone SOA in Authority, but emit its original TTL. RFC 2308 requires the SOA TTL to be the minimum of SOA TTL and SOA.MINIMUM. | No |
| §3.1 Name space specifications and terminology | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:140-151,173-182` | **Violation**: RFC 1034 §3.1 says preserve received name case; RFC 4343 §4.2 says not to convert case when master-file input creates names. Zone construction lowercases every owner and selected embedded domain names before storing them, losing input case. | No |
| §3.1.3.4 Including NSEC RRs: Wildcard No Data Response | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:855-867,931-949` | **Partial**: For wildcard NODATA with DO set, Pharos includes the source wildcard's NSEC but not an NSEC proving no closer match exists. | No |
| §3.2 Glue for Sibling Domain Name Servers | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:963-982` | **Partial**: RFC 9471 says servers SHOULD include available sibling-domain glue. The filter at line 966 excludes NS targets outside the delegation owner's subtree, even when their address records are available in the parent zone. No plugin README or compatibility entry records this omission. | No |
| §3.3.1 Closest Encloser and the Source of Synthesis | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:541-580` | **Violation**: The closest-encloser search starts at QNAME's parent. An empty non-terminal QNAME is therefore skipped, allowing synthesis from an ancestor wildcard. | No |
| §3.6 Resource Records — zero TTL | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/mod.rs:593-618,625-652`; `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/setup.rs:49-56` | **Violation**: The cache applies its configured minimum TTL before checking eligibility. With the default five-second minimum, a zero-TTL response can be rewritten and cached, contrary to RFC 1034 §3.6 and RFC 8767 §4. | No |
| §3.6.2 Aliases and canonical names — CNAME exclusivity | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:135-188` | **Violation**: Static zone construction does not reject a CNAME coexisting with ordinary data at the same owner. RFC 2181 §10.1 permits only the CNAME and specified DNSSEC records there. Dynamic-update paths check conflicts, but static zones do not. | No |
| §3.6.2 Aliases and canonical names — CNAME restart QTYPEs | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/template/mod.rs:665-675` | **Partial**: Template follows a CNAME target only for A and AAAA queries; RFC 1034 requires restarting every non-CNAME query at the target. | Yes |
| §3.6.2 Aliases and canonical names — bounded CNAME chains | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/rewrite/mod.rs:2171-2173,2219-2232` | **Partial**: Rewrite follows CNAME targets only through its self-lookup depth limit; later targets are skipped, so long chains are not fully followed. The README documents the CoreDNS-compatible depth limit. | Yes |
| §3.6.2 Aliases and canonical names — loops | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:599-627,664-687` | **Violation**: Self-loops return SERVFAIL, but multi-name loops hit the chain limit and can return the partial CNAME chain with NOERROR instead of signaling an error. The nearby citation claims loops are detected but does not cover multi-name cycles. | Yes |
| §3.6.2 Aliases and canonical names — matching class | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:612-627`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/autopath/mod.rs:338-343` | **Violation**: Zone CNAME chasing ignores class, and autopath emits CNAME class IN regardless of QCLASS. Either path can return or follow a CNAME with the wrong class. | No |
| §3.6.2 Aliases and canonical names — rewrite-loop errors | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/rewrite/mod.rs:2166-2173,2219-2232`, `crates/pharos-plugins/tests/functional/rewrite.rs:409-457` | **Violation**: A CNAME loop that reaches the depth limit returns the last successful response with NOERROR instead of signaling an error. The README documents the limit, not this response behavior. | No |
| §3.7.1 Standard queries — QCLASS matching | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:277-287,639-666,796-810`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/k8s_external/mod.rs:181-207,258-275,335-347,858-899` | **Violation**: Zone RR lookup ignores QCLASS, and k8s_external emits IN records for non-IN queries. A query can receive records from the wrong class. | No |
| §3.7.1 Standard queries — QCLASS=* authority | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:705-709` | **Violation**: The response sets AA without checking QCLASS. RFC 1034 says responses to QCLASS=* queries can never be authoritative. | No |
| §4 Behavior of DNS Responders | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/any/mod.rs:10-17,32-47` | **Deviation**: `Any` synthesizes HINFO without checking whether QNAME exists, bypassing normal NXDOMAIN and wildcard processing for nonexistent names. The README documents that HINFO is synthesized before consulting a Backend. | Yes |
| §4 Serving Stale Data | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/setup.rs:60,139-179`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/store.rs:179-187` | **Partial**: `serve_stale` defaults stale TTL to zero, and stale responses then carry TTL 0. RFC 8767 requires stale records to be returned with a TTL greater than zero. | No |
| §4.1 DNS Output Case Preservation | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:147-151,794-810` | **Violation**: Zone loading lowercases owner names, then emits those names in answers. This loses original case, contrary to RFC 4343's output-preservation requirement. | No |
| §4.2 Answer with a Synthesized HINFO RRset | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/any/mod.rs:10-17,32-47` | **Deviation**: The plugin synthesizes HINFO even when QNAME owns a CNAME. RFC 8482 §4.2 only permits this synthesis when no CNAME is present. The README records this CoreDNS-compatible behavior. | Yes |
| §4.2 Answer with a Synthesized HINFO RRset | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/any/mod.rs:37-47` | **Deviation**: For DO queries at signed names, the synthesized HINFO has no RRSIG, contrary to the MUST requirement. The README records that this plugin cannot include an RRSIG. | Yes |
| §4.2 Answer with a Synthesized HINFO RRset | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/any/mod.rs:37-47` | **Violation**: The synthesized HINFO uses CPU `ANY obsoleted` and OS `See RFC 8482`; RFC 8482 recommends CPU `RFC8482` and an empty OS field. | No |
| §4.2 Answer with a Synthesized HINFO RRset | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/any/mod.rs:39-45`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/any/setup.rs:1-12` | **Violation**: HINFO TTL is fixed at 8482 and cannot be configured, despite RFC 8482 recommending operator-configurable TTL. | No |
| §4.2 DNS Input Case Preservation | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:147-151,173-174` | **Violation**: Zone loading lowercases owner names and selected name-bearing RDATA instead of preserving case for newly created names. | No |
| §4.2 How the database is divided into zones | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:277-286`, `crates/pharos-server/src/admission.rs:53-56` | **Violation**: Zone lookup keys records by owner and type, not class. When non-IN queries are admitted, records can cross class boundaries instead of using separate class databases. | No |
| §4.2.1 Technical considerations — zone apex records | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:135-190`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/update.rs:23-83` | **Violation**: Static and dynamic zone construction require an apex SOA but accept no apex NS RRset. RFC 1034 describes both the zone-server NS records and one SOA as top-node data. | No |
| §4.3.1 Queries and responses — QCLASS selection | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:277-287,639-666,796-810`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/local/mod.rs:120-132,169-195` | **Violation**: RR lookup ignores QCLASS and returns stored records of their configured class. A query for another class can receive IN data, although QCLASS identifies the desired class. | No |
| §4.3.1 Queries and responses — recursion availability | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:698-721`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/local/mod.rs:161-165`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/any/mod.rs:28-32`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/chaos/mod.rs:106-111`, `crates/pharos-server/src/admission.rs:77-86` | **Violation**: These response builders omit RA. When `forward` is configured, Pharos can provide recursive service, but local authoritative, local-zone, CHAOS, ANY, and error responses do not report that availability. | No |
| §4.3.2 Algorithm — ANY handling | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/any/mod.rs:18-47` | **Deviation**: The handler synthesizes HINFO without the zone/cache lookup needed for name errors, referrals, or CNAME processing. The plugin README records this CoreDNS-compatible pre-backend response. | Yes |
| §4.3.2 Algorithm — Kubernetes CNAME response | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/kubernetes/mod.rs:1085-1097` | **Violation**: For A/AAAA questions, the CNAME is emitted only when self-lookup succeeds. The algorithm requires copying the CNAME before following its canonical name. The citation at line 1087 claims the CNAME is returned whatever the QTYPE, which the code contradicts for A/AAAA. | No |
| §4.3.2 Algorithm — RA flag | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/header/mod.rs:61-72,85-90` | **Deviation**: Header rules can make RA misstate recursive-service availability. The README describes configurable response-flag changes. | Yes |
| §4.3.2 Algorithm — RD flag | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/header/mod.rs:61-72,85-87` | **Deviation**: Header rules can set RD before later plugins process a query whose original RD bit was clear. The README describes query-flag rewriting as intentional header-plugin behavior. | Yes |
| §4.3.2 Algorithm — apex name with absent QTYPE | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:541-544,676-688`, `crates/pharos-plugins/tests/functional/file_coredns.rs:1007-1015` | **Violation**: An apex with SOA and NS exists, but lookup can return NXDOMAIN for an absent QTYPE. A matched name with no matching answer RRset must return NOERROR, not a name error. | No |
| §4.3.2 Algorithm — authoritative local name errors | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/local/mod.rs:135,161-166` | **Violation**: The local plugin returns NXDOMAIN for missing names in its locally served zones but never sets AA. RFC 1034 requires an authoritative name error for a missing name in authoritative data. | No |
| §4.3.2 Algorithm — missing hosts names | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/hosts/mod.rs:303-316` | **Violation**: When no mapped records match and fallthrough does not apply, the plugin returns SERVFAIL. RFC 1034 requires an authoritative name error when the name does not exist and no wildcard answers it. | No |
| §4.3.2 Algorithm — rewrite CNAME depth limit | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/rewrite/mod.rs:2171-2173,2219-2232` | **Partial**: Rewrite follows a CNAME with self-lookup only through depth 8; deeper targets are skipped instead of continuing the canonical-name lookup. The README records the CoreDNS-compatible limit. | Yes |
| §4.3.3 Wildcards | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:563-580` | **Violation**: An existing empty non-terminal has `exists == true` but `exact == false`; lookup can still synthesize from its parent wildcard. RFC 4592 §2.2.3 confirms empty non-terminals exist and block wildcard synthesis. | No |
| §4.3.3 Wildcards | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/mod.rs:153-169,625-647` | **Violation**: Explicit `*` labels in QNAMEs should not be cached. The cache has no such exclusion and can store matching positive responses. Held erratum 5316 is not normative under this audit brief. | No |
| §4.3.5 Zone maintenance and transfers — Kubernetes serial updates | `crates/pharos-kubernetes/src/store.rs:1322-1336,1381-1390,1540-1547` | **Deviation**: Service IP and endpoint target-name changes signal readers but do not advance the SOA serial. The Kubernetes plugin README records these CoreDNS-compatible cases; ADR 0002 permits compatibility where deployments depend on CoreDNS. | Yes |
| §4.3.5 Zone maintenance and transfers — Namespace serial updates | `crates/pharos-kubernetes/src/store.rs:870-877,1473-1498` | **Violation**: With `require_namespace`, adding or removing a Namespace can expose or hide DNS data, but these updates signal readers without advancing the SOA serial. No justification appears in the plugin README or ADRs. | No |
| §4.3.5 Zone maintenance and transfers — expiry after failed SOA check | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/secondary/update.rs:208-210,227-232,316-328` | **Violation**: A non-NOERROR SOA response leaves `retry_active` false, so expiry ticks never expire the zone after EXPIRE elapses. | No |
| §4.3.5 Zone maintenance and transfers — retry after failed SOA check | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/secondary/update.rs:208-210,227-232,334-357` | **Violation**: A non-NOERROR SOA response from the last or only primary becomes `Ok(false)`, restarting REFRESH instead of scheduling the required RETRY after an incomplete check. | No |
| §4.3.5 Zone maintenance and transfers — serial resolution | `crates/pharos-kubernetes/src/store.rs:862-868` | **Deviation**: SOA serial uses Unix time in whole seconds, so two changes in one second can reuse a value. The Kubernetes plugin README records this CoreDNS-compatible limitation. | Yes |
| §4.3.5 Zone maintenance and transfers — zero SOA timers | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/secondary/update.rs:258-265,317-320` | **Partial**: SOA timers are read, but `.max(1)` changes a zero-second REFRESH, RETRY, or EXPIRE interval to one second. | No |
| §5 Caching Negative Answers | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/mod.rs:767-779` | **Violation**: NXDOMAIN cache keys include QTYPE. RFC 2308 requires an NXDOMAIN entry to be reusable for the same QNAME and QCLASS across query types. | No |
| §5 Caching Negative Answers | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/mod.rs:593-618`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/setup.rs:49-56,90-113` | **Violation**: `denial_min_ttl` can raise a negative TTL above the SOA TTL or MINIMUM-derived limit, including the default five seconds. This lets the cache retain a negative answer past its permitted lifetime. | No |
| §5.2 RRSet TTLs | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:145-188,445-457` | **Violation**: Zone construction accepts records in one RRSet with different TTLs, then sends their original TTLs. RFC 2181 §5.2 prohibits authoritative servers from sending an RRSet with differing TTLs. | No |
| §5.3.2 Resolver resources | `crates/pharos-plugins/plugin.cfg:50-65`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/mod.rs:163-213` | **Violation**: Cache runs before the authoritative `file` plugin and returns cache hits before checking its data. A cached answer can therefore override current zone data; the cache bypass only covers dynamic-update zones. RFC 2181 §5.4.1 also ranks zone-file data above cached data. | No |
| §5.3.3 Algorithm | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/mod.rs:767-779`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/store.rs:157-161` | **Partial**: NXDOMAIN responses are cached, but the cache key and hit check include QTYPE. RFC 2308 §5 requires NXDOMAIN reuse for the same QNAME and QCLASS regardless of QTYPE. | No |
| §5.3.3 Algorithm — configured resolver | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/forward/resolve.rs:83-104` | **Partial**: The configured-resolver path extracts A/AAAA records without checking the response name or RCODE and does not follow a CNAME-only answer. It can accept unrelated address data or fail valid aliases. | No |
| §6 Negative Answers from the Cache | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/store.rs:179-187`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/setup.rs:238-243` | **Violation**: With `keepttl`, every cached negative response gets its original TTL on each hit instead of a TTL reduced by cache age. | No |
| §6 Zone Integrity | `crates/pharos-server/src/transfer.rs:195-217`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/secondary/update.rs:135-154` | **Violation**: The client treats a final SOA of any contents as successful completion, then atomically publishes the resulting Zone. A malformed transfer can therefore replace the valid snapshot. | No |
| §6.1 Zone authority | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:189-191` | **Partial**: Zone construction requires an apex SOA but accepts a zone without apex NS. RFC 2181 §6.1 requires both records in every zone. | No |

## RFC 1035: Domain names - implementation and specification

| Section | Code location | Explanation | Documented |
|---|---|---|---|
| §2.2 Common configurations | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/secondary/mod.rs:60-65`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/secondary/setup.rs:90-100` | **Partial**: Pharos can retrieve and serve configured secondary zones, but it permits primary zones without a secondary and does not verify that every zone has redundant support. | No |
| §2.3.4 Size limits | `crates/pharos-corefile/src/key.rs:35,108-156` | **Violation**: The early limit checks presentation-byte length, rejecting legal names whose decimal escapes encode fewer wire octets. | No |
| §3.1 Name space definitions | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:145-151,173-183` | **Violation**: RFC 4343 §§4.1–4.2 require preserving ASCII case on output and when creating name nodes. Zone loading lowercases owners and name-bearing RDATA before storage, losing source case. | No |
| §3.2.1 Format | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/mod.rs:593-650` | **Deviation**: When configured minimum TTL is positive, cache raises zero-TTL records to that minimum and caches them. The cache README records this CoreDNS-compatible behavior; RFC 1035 §3.2.1 says zero-TTL records are transaction-only and must not be cached. RFC 2181 §7.2 only removes the requirement to send SOA records with zero TTL. | Yes |
| §3.2.1 Format | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:135-188,445-457` | **Violation**: RFC 2181 §5.2 requires all records in an RRset to have the same TTL. Zone loading accepts differing TTLs, and `write_set` emits each stored TTL unchanged. | No |
| §3.2.1 Format | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:192-223,445-457` | **Violation**: RFC 2181 §5 says servers should suppress duplicate RRs. Zone indexing retains duplicate records and `write_set` emits each copy. | No |
| §3.2.3 QTYPE values | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/any/mod.rs:15-47` | **Deviation**: RFC 8482 §4.2 permits synthesized HINFO only when no CNAME exists at QNAME. The plugin returns HINFO without checking for a CNAME; its README records this behavior. | Yes |
| §3.2.3 QTYPE values | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/any/mod.rs:18-48` | **Deviation**: RFC 8482 §4.2 requires a valid RRSIG when DO=1 for a known signed zone. The plugin returns HINFO without a signature; its README documents this limitation. | Yes |
| §3.2.3 QTYPE values | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/any/mod.rs:6-11,37-46`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/any/setup.rs:1-12` | **Partial**: RFC 8482 §4.2 says the synthesized HINFO TTL should be configurable by the operator. The plugin hard-codes TTL 8482 and exposes no setting. | No |
| §3.3.1 CNAME RDATA format (RFC 2181 §10.1) | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:201-223`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/file/tests.rs:169-180` | **Violation**: Zone accepts two CNAME records with different targets at one owner; construction stores both in the owner/type list. | No |
| §3.3.1 CNAME RDATA format (RFC 2181 §10.1) | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:135-223` | **Violation**: Zone construction permits CNAME records alongside other RR types, despite RFC 2181's prohibition except for SIG, NXT, and KEY. | No |
| §3.3.2 HINFO RDATA format | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone/rdata.rs:167-193` | **Violation**: A one-field HINFO master-file record is accepted and an empty OS string is supplied, although HINFO requires both CPU and OS character-strings. | No |
| §3.3.2 HINFO RDATA format (RFC 8482 §4.2 CPU field) | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/any/mod.rs:37-46` | **Violation**: Synthesized HINFO uses CPU `ANY obsoleted`, not RFC 8482's recommended `RFC8482`; `any/README.md` documents that HINFO is synthesized, not these field values. | No |
| §3.3.2 HINFO RDATA format (RFC 8482 §4.2 OS field) | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/any/mod.rs:37-46` | **Deviation**: Synthesized HINFO uses OS `See RFC 8482`, not RFC 8482's recommended null string; `any/README.md` records the CoreDNS-compatible synthesis. | Yes |
| §3.3.3 MB RDATA format | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:984-996` | **Violation**: MB requires additional-section processing that looks up A records for MADNAME. The target selection omits MB. | No |
| §3.3.4 MD RDATA format | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:984-996` | **Violation**: MD requires additional-section processing that looks up A records for MADNAME. The target selection omits MD. | No |
| §3.3.4 MD RDATA format | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone/rdata/legacy.rs:34-36` | **Violation**: The section recommends rejecting MD master-file records or converting them to MX with preference 0. The parser accepts MD unchanged. | No |
| §3.3.5 MF RDATA format | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:984-996` | **Violation**: MF requires additional-section processing that looks up A records for MADNAME. The target selection omits MF. | No |
| §3.3.5 MF RDATA format | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone/rdata/legacy.rs:34-36` | **Violation**: As written, this section recommends rejecting MD master-file records or converting them to MX with preference 10; the parser accepts MD unchanged. Erratum 3421 proposes changing MD to MF but is Held for Document Update, so it is not normative here. | No |
| §3.3.8 MR RDATA format | `crates/pharos-proto/src/rdata.rs:9-32,963-965`, `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone/rdata/legacy.rs:33-37` | **Partial**: Zone parsing accepts MR as one domain name, and generic RDATA can relocate it, but typed `Rdata::parse_in_message` rejects MR through `unsupported_name_type`. | No |
| §3.3.9 MX RDATA format | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:145-187,984-1022` | **Violation**: RFC 2181 §10.3 prohibits an MX EXCHANGE alias, but Zone loading does not reject targets that are CNAME owners. | No |
| §3.3.9 MX RDATA format | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:984-1022` | **Partial**: RFC 2181 §10.3 requires address records for MX targets; code neither verifies that RRset nor looks up out-of-zone exchanges for additional processing. | No |
| §3.3.9 MX RDATA format | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone/rdata.rs:409-501` | **Violation**: Generic RDATA for known MX type accepts missing fields, inserts a zero preference, and omits the required exchange name. RFC 3597 §5 requires further processing as a known type. | No |
| §3.3.11 NS RDATA format | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:145-187,984-1022` | **Violation**: RFC 2181 §10.3 prohibits an NSDNAME alias, but Zone loading does not reject targets that are CNAME owners. | No |
| §3.3.11 NS RDATA format | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:984-1022` | **Partial**: RFC 2181 §10.3 requires address records for NS targets; code neither verifies that RRset nor looks up out-of-zone apex targets, though referral glue searches the delegated Zone. | No |
| §3.3.12 PTR RDATA format | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:145-187` | **Violation**: RFC 2181 §10.2 requires a canonical PTR target, but Zone loading does not reject a target that is a CNAME owner. | No |
| §3.3.13 SOA RDATA format | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:445-457, 827-853` | **Violation**: RFC 2308 §3 requires negative-answer SOA TTL to be the minimum of SOA TTL and SOA.MINIMUM, but `write_set` copies SOA TTL unchanged. RFC 2308 §4 deprecates applying MINIMUM as a floor to positive RR TTLs. | No |
| §3.4.1 A RDATA format | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/whoami/mod.rs:24-30,71-80` | **Violation**: A records are Internet-class data, but `whoami` copies a non-IN QCLASS into its A record. | No |
| §3.4.1 A RDATA format | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone/rdata.rs:409-477` | **Violation**: Generic known-type A RDATA accepts an empty field and emits no 32-bit address. RFC 3597 §5 requires further processing as a known type. | No |
| §3.4.2 WKS RDATA format | `crates/pharos-proto/src/codes.rs:97-188`, `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone.rs:912-920`, `crates/pharos-proto/src/rdata.rs:963-965` | **Partial**: WKS has no mnemonic or typed RDATA support, so its address/protocol/bitmap fields and legacy master-file form are unavailable. Opaque type-11 RDATA can still pass through using RFC 3597 generic syntax (`TYPE11 \# ...`). | No |
| §4 Standards Action | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/mod.rs:591-618,625-652`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/README.md:28-30` | **Deviation**: The cache raises a zero record TTL to the configured minimum, then inserts the Response; default minimum is 5 seconds. RFC 8767 retains the rule that zero-TTL records must not be cached. The README records this behavior but gives no standards rationale. | Yes |
| §4 Standards Action | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/store.rs:163-187`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/mod.rs:218-290` | **Violation**: With stale serving enabled and default `stale_ttl = 0`, the cache returns expired records with TTL 0. RFC 8767 requires a positive TTL on every stale record in a Response. | No |
| §4 Standards Action | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/setup.rs:53-56,87-93,259-263` | **Partial**: The default cache TTL cap is 3,600 seconds, but configuration accepts a cap up to `u32::MAX` seconds. This can exceed RFC 8767's recommended 604,800-second cap. | No |
| §4.1.1 CD | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dnssec/mod.rs:104-106` | **Violation**: authoritative DNSKEY responses copy CD from the query. RFC 4035 §3.1.6 says a security-aware authoritative server SHOULD clear CD; the code comment cites that section but claims the opposite. | No |
| §4.1.1 Header section format | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/forward/mod.rs:345-363` | **Violation**: An upstream response mismatch is mapped to FORMERR, which means the server could not interpret the client query; the query was already parsed. | No |
| §4.1.1 Header section format | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/minimal/mod.rs:50-58` | **Violation**: The plugin clears section counts but leaves question and answer octets after the declared sections. | No |
| §4.1.1 Header section format | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/secondary/update.rs:197-210` | **Violation**: SOA response handling checks ID and RCODE but accepts QR=0 query messages as responses. | No |
| §4.1.1 Header section format | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dnssec/signing.rs:151-157`, `crates/pharos-plugins/tests/functional/dnssec.rs:592-626`, `crates/pharos-plugins/tests/allocations/dnssec_allocations.rs:131-138` | **Deviation**: The DNSSEC black-lie path clears NXDOMAIN to NOERROR and returns synthetic NSEC data; the plugin README and ADR 0002 document CoreDNS compatibility. | Yes |
| §4.1.1 Header section format | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/header/mod.rs:61-72,85-90`, `crates/pharos-plugins/tests/functional/header.rs:63-70,120-181` | **Violation**: Header rules can set AA in queries or responses without authority; the header README records only in-place flag edits, and ADR 0002 gives no evidence of reliance on this. | No |
| §4.1.1 Header section format | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/header/mod.rs:61-72,85-90`, `crates/pharos-plugins/tests/functional/header.rs:65,120-164` | **Violation**: Header rules can rewrite RD instead of copying its query value; the header README records only in-place flag edits, and ADR 0002 gives no evidence of reliance on this. | No |
| §4.1.1 Header section format | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/header/mod.rs:61-72,85-90`, `crates/pharos-plugins/tests/functional/header.rs:63-69,170-181` | **Violation**: Header rules can force RA without confirming recursive service; the header README records only in-place flag edits, and ADR 0002 gives no evidence of reliance on this. | No |
| §4.1.1 Header section format | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/erratic/mod.rs:45,109-110,117-120` | **Violation**: `erratic` sets TC by a query-counter mask and can stop adding records without TC, independent of the transmission limit; no justification is recorded. | No |
| §4.1.1 Header section format | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/nsid/mod.rs:121-126`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/nsid/README.md:10` | **Deviation**: NSID drops records after OPT and sets TC to avoid invalidating compression pointers; the README records this limitation. | Yes |
| §4.1.1 QDCOUNT | `crates/pharos-server/src/admission.rs:39-43` | **Partial**: RFC 9619 permits OPCODE=QUERY with QDCOUNT zero, but the listener returns FORMERR unless QDCOUNT is exactly one. RFC 9619 requires FORMERR for counts above one; its explicit MUST NOT for zero applies to firewalls. | No |
| §4.1.1 RCODE | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/k8s_external/lookup.rs:59-62` | **Violation**: An empty relative name is a valid DNS name, not evidence of a server failure, but the parser returns SERVFAIL. | No |
| §4.1.1 RCODE | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/kubernetes/mod.rs:804-838,849-863,1083-1113`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/k8s_external/mod.rs:795-820,197-205,497-528` | **Partial**: Responses that follow a CNAME include target records but retain the original lookup RCODE, even when the target lookup returns an error. RFC 6604 §3 requires the final response RCODE to reflect the final query cycle. | No |
| §4.1.1 TC | `crates/pharos-proto/src/builder.rs:328-334`, `crates/pharos-server/src/outcome.rs:267-302` | **Partial**: TC is set whenever any record is omitted for size, including optional Additional data. RFC 2181 §9 says not to set TC merely because extra information did not fit; the builder also has no RRset-level completeness check. | Yes |
| §4.1.1 Z | `crates/pharos-server/src/admission.rs:28-45`, `crates/pharos-proto/src/builder.rs:143-156` | **Partial**: the listener accepts queries with reserved Z bit set, and the public builder accepts raw flags containing it. RFC 1035 requires this bit to be zero in queries and responses. | No |
| §4.1.2 Question section format; §4.1.3 Resource record format | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/local/mod.rs:120-130,161-194` | **Violation**: Answer selection ignores QCLASS and emits records as class IN, so non-IN queries receive records from the wrong class. | No |
| §4.1.3 Resource record format | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/mod.rs:593-620,625-652`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/setup.rs:49-69` | **Deviation**: TTL-zero responses are raised to the default five-second minimum and cached. This follows CoreDNS behavior, recorded in ADR 0002 and the cache README. | Yes |
| §4.1.4 Message compression | `crates/pharos-proto/src/name.rs:520-532` | **Violation**: Name parsing rejects valid backward-pointer chains after 255 hops, although RFC 1035 sets no hop limit. | No |
| §4.1.4 Message compression | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone/rdata.rs:727-752`, `crates/pharos-proto/tests/zone_differential_live.rs:886-910` | **Violation**: Generic known-type RDATA accepts forward compression pointers; RFC 1035 permits pointers only to prior occurrences. | No |
| §4.2.1 UDP usage | `crates/pharos-server/src/udp_linux.rs:22-52,57-71`, `crates/pharos-server/src/udp.rs:133-143,462-472,566-578`, `crates/pharos-server/src/lib.rs:877-905` | **Partial**: Wildcard-bound UDP listeners retain the peer address but not the request's destination address, then reply through the wildcard socket. On a multihomed host, this does not ensure the reply source matches the query destination as RFC 2181 §4.1 requires. | No |
| §4.2.2 TCP usage | `crates/pharos-server/src/lib.rs:946-980` | **Partial**: Shutdown aborts unfinished tasks after five seconds, so outstanding TCP requests may be dropped instead of being satisfied before close. | No |
| §5.1 Format | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone.rs:443-474` | **Partial**: TTL defaults persist, but omitted classes reset to IN instead of using the last explicitly stated class. | No |
| §5.1 Format | `crates/pharos-proto/src/codes.rs:97-116`, `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone.rs:913-921` | **Partial**: The parser uses standard type mnemonics, but `WKS` (type 11) is missing; only generic `TYPE11` can represent it. | No |
| §5.1 Format | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone.rs:587-601`, `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone/rdata.rs:786-809,872-899`, `crates/pharos-proto/tests/zone_strictness.rs:199-208` | **Violation**: Decimal escapes above 255 are cast to `u8` and wrap (`\999` becomes octet 231), rather than representing one octet. | No |
| §5.1 Format | `crates/pharos-proto/src/rdata.rs:347-370,1346-1359` | **Violation**: Display formatting decodes literal backslash-digit and backslash-dot bytes, changing CAA/URI wire values instead of escaping each raw octet. | No |
| §5.1 Format | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone/rdata/advanced.rs:190-202`, `crates/pharos-proto/tests/zone_advanced.rs:52-54` | **Violation**: TKEY rejects a relative algorithm name even when an origin is available to resolve it. | No |
| §5.1 Format | `crates/pharos-proto/tests/zone_final_line.rs:224-240,265-270` | **Violation**: The lexer rejects escaped blanks in HINFO character-strings, although `\X` quotes any non-digit character. | No |
| §5.1 Format | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/template/mod.rs:627-636`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/template/README.md:29` | **Deviation**: Rendered zone text cannot resolve `$INCLUDE` files during a query; the plugin README records this limitation. | Yes |
| §5.2 Use of master files to define zones | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:104-120, 135-198` | **Violation**: The loader does not require all records to have one class; explicit mixed classes are accepted. | No |
| §5.2 Use of master files to define zones | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:104-120,144-198`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/sign/signer.rs:58-70` | **Violation**: Zone construction and the signer silently retain the last SOA instead of rejecting multiple SOAs. The signer mirrors CoreDNS, but the general Zone path has no recorded justification. | No |
| §5.2 Use of master files to define zones | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:189-191` | **Partial**: RFC 2181 §6.1 requires apex NS and SOA records. The loader requires SOA but accepts zones without apex NS. | No |
| §5.2 Use of master files to define zones | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:963-982`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:104-120` | **Partial**: Referrals include available in-bailiwick A/AAAA glue, but zone loading does not check that required glue exists. | No |
| §5.2 Use of master files to define zones | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:135-230`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/transfer.rs:83-103` | **Violation**: Zone loading accepts owners outside the zone without checking that they are glue; AXFR can then emit those records. RFC 2181 §6.1 also limits authoritative zone data to names in that zone. | No |
| §6.1.2 Database | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/secondary/mod.rs:37-46,52-55,93-100`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/secondary/catalog.rs:133-135`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/store.rs:73-80` | **Partial**: Master-file-backed Zones and database-configured dynamic updates have durable sources, but secondary Zone snapshots and received catalog membership live only in memory. A restart with an unavailable upstream leaves those Zones unavailable until transfer succeeds. | No |
| §6.2 Standard query processing | `crates/pharos-server/src/admission.rs:53-55`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/lib.rs:506-511`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:705-706`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/hosts/mod.rs:284-328`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/template/mod.rs:357-361,692-700` | **Violation**: Zone, hosts, and template handlers can answer QCLASS=ANY with only selected-class data while setting AA, without guaranteeing coverage of all classes. | No |
| §6.2 Standard query processing | `crates/pharos-server/src/outcome.rs:244-267,297-299` | **Violation**: When scrubbing removes records only from Additional, code still sets TC. RFC 2181 §9 updates this rule: omit extra Additional data without TC when required RRsets remain complete. | No |
| §6.3 Zone refresh and reload processing | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/secondary/mod.rs:198-204`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/file/mod.rs:75-115,381-384` | **Partial**: Unavailable secondary data returns SERVFAIL, while failed file reloads retain stale data or return SERVFAIL; RFC 1035 says to answer as if the server did not possess the zone. | No |
| §7.3 Processing responses | `crates/pharos-proto/src/message.rs:203-239`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/proxy/mod.rs:522-567` | **Partial**: Message parsing checks section bounds and record framing, but leaves type-specific RDATA validation lazy. The proxy accepts a parsed response without validating every RR's RDATA. | No |
| §7.3 Processing responses | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/forward/resolve.rs:83-88` | **Partial**: The forward resolver checks the response ID but accepts a query-shaped message where a response is required. | No |
| §7.4 Using the cache | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/mod.rs:214-291` | **Violation**: A cache hit returns before `Next` runs, so cached data can take precedence over authoritative data in the remaining chain. | No |
| §7.4 Using the cache | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/store.rs:94-118` | **Partial**: Replacing an entry never merges RRsets, but the store does not track authority or trust. It can replace more-trustworthy cached data with a less-trustworthy response, contrary to RFC 2181 §5.4.1. | No |
| §7.4 Using the cache | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/mod.rs:595-609, 648-652`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/store.rs:317-324` | **Violation**: The cache stores every RR from a matching response, including unrelated authority and additional records, without filtering unrequested data or its reliability. | No |
| §7.4 Using the cache | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/setup.rs:49-66`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/mod.rs:591-618, 651-652` | **Deviation**: The default five-second minimum raises received TTLs below five seconds, including zero, before caching. RFC 8767 §7 says zero-TTL data must not be cached; the README records this TTL-raising behavior. | Yes |
| §7.4 Using the cache | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/setup.rs:139-148`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/store.rs:179-187` | **Violation**: `serve_stale` defaults its response TTL to zero, and expired records receive that TTL unless configured otherwise. RFC 8767 §4 requires stale records to have a TTL greater than zero. | No |
| §7.4 Using the cache | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/mod.rs:767-779` | **Partial**: The cache key includes QTYPE, so an NXDOMAIN cached for one type cannot answer another type for the same QNAME and QCLASS, as RFC 2308 §5 requires. | No |

## RFC 1183: New DNS RR Definitions

| Section | Code location | Explanation | Documented |
|---|---|---|---|
| §1 AFS Data Base location — additional section processing | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:984-1023` | **Missing**: Zone responses add address glue for MX, NS, SRV, SVCB, and HTTPS targets, but not AFSDB hostnames; RFC 1183 §1 requires type A additional section processing for AFSDB. | No |
| §2.2 The Responsible Person RR | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone.rs:559-564`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:145-188`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:222`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:454-457` | **Partial**: Zone loading accepts multiple RP records at one owner with different TTLs, then serves each TTL unchanged. RFC 1183 says such records should have identical TTLs. | No |
| §3.1 The X25 RR — PSDN address syntax | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone/rdata/legacy.rs:58-67` | **Violation**: Parser accepts arbitrary strings; it does not require decimal digits or a four-digit DNIC at the start. | No |
| §3.1 The X25 RR — national prefixes | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone/rdata/legacy.rs:58-67` | **Violation**: Parser does not reject national prefixes, despite the RFC's MUST NOT. | No |
| §3.2 The ISDN RR — address format | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone/rdata/legacy.rs:69-84` | **Violation**: Parser accepts addresses without an E.163 country-code prefix. | No |
| §3.2 The ISDN RR — field count | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone/rdata/legacy.rs:69-97` | **Violation**: Parser accepts extra fields and joins them into the optional subaddress instead of rejecting them. | No |
| §3.2 The ISDN RR — subaddress format | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone/rdata/legacy.rs:85-96` | **Violation**: Parser accepts non-hexadecimal subaddresses. | No |
| §3.3 The Route Through RR — additional processing | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:984-1023` | **Missing**: Zone responses add address glue for MX, NS, and SRV targets, but not RT's intermediate host; RT requires A, X25, and ISDN additional processing. | No |
| §3.3 The Route Through RR — wire RDATA support | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone/rdata/legacy.rs:45-50`, `crates/pharos-proto/src/rdata.rs:8-32,963-965`, `crates/pharos-proto/src/message.rs:87-89,178-185` | **Partial**: Zone parsing and compressed-name discovery support RT's preference/name layout, but typed RDATA parsing rejects RT; `Record::data()` and message rewriting then fail. | No |

## RFC 1348: DNS NSAP RRs

| Section | Code location | Explanation | Documented |
|---|---|---|---|
| §NSAP RR (mnemonic NSAP, type 22, `<length> <NSAP-address>` presentation) | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone.rs:912-921`, `crates/pharos-proto/src/codes.rs:97-122` | **Partial**: type 22 is served through generic RFC 3597 RDATA (`TYPE22`), but the `NSAP` mnemonic is not in the type table or zone parser, and the `<length>` + ASCII-digit presentation is not parsed. Same gap as RFC 1706 §5 mnemonic and RFC 1706 §7 rows. | No |

## RFC 1706: DNS NSAP Resource Records

| Section | Code location | Explanation | Documented |
|---|---|---|---|
| §4 Structure of NSAPs — NSel | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone/rdata.rs:88-100,410-455` | **Violation**: Generic `TYPE22` RDATA is accepted as opaque bytes, so Pharos can publish an NSAP whose final NSel octet is nonzero. The code does not enforce the required zero NSel value. | No |
| §5 The NSAP RR — class | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone.rs:444-465,559-565`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:145-188` | **Violation**: RFC 1706 defines NSAP records with class IN, but the zone parser accepts other classes and zone construction retains them for type 22. | No |
| §5 The NSAP RR — mnemonic | `crates/pharos-proto/src/codes.rs:97-121`, `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone.rs:912-918` | **Partial**: Wire type 22 works through generic `TYPE22` handling, but the type table and zone parser do not recognize the defined `NSAP` mnemonic. | No |
| §5 The NSAP RR — zero TTL | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/setup.rs:54-56`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/mod.rs:593-618,625-628` | **Deviation**: The default positive-cache minimum raises a zero TTL to five seconds, allowing the NSAP record to be cached. ADR 0002 allows CoreDNS-compatible behavior, and the cache README's differential-scenario note records the raise to the configured minimum. | Yes |
| §7 Master File Format | `crates/pharos-proto/src/codes.rs:97-122`, `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone.rs:912-921`, `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone/rdata.rs:88-100` | **Partial**: Type 22 can use RFC 3597 generic RDATA, but Pharos lacks the `NSAP` mnemonic and RFC 1706's `0x` hexadecimal presentation syntax, including optional periods. | No |

## RFC 1712: DNS Encoding of Geographical Location

| Section | Code location | Explanation | Documented |
|---|---|---|---|
| §3 RDATA Format — LATITUDE | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone/rdata/legacy.rs:99-107` | **Violation**: GPOS parsing accepts any value that parses as `f64`; it does not enforce the specified -180..180 range. | No |
| §3 RDATA Format — LONGITUDE | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone/rdata/legacy.rs:99-107` | **Violation**: GPOS parsing accepts any value that parses as `f64`; it does not enforce the specified -90..90 range. | No |
| §4 The GPOS RR — field length limit | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone/rdata/legacy.rs:106`, `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone/rdata.rs:903-907` | **Partial**: Each field is stored as one character-string capped at 255 octets by its length octet (RFC 1035 §3.3), so a 256-character field that §4 allows is rejected. | No |
| §4 The GPOS RR — numeric values and ranges | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone/rdata/legacy.rs:99-107` | **Partial**: The parser requires three fields and parses each as `f64`, but accepts values outside the stated longitude and latitude ranges. | No |
| §4 The GPOS RR — optional owner, TTL, and class | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone.rs:434-475, 555-558` | **Partial**: An omitted owner and TTL inherit prior values, but class resets to `IN` for each record instead of inheriting the last defined class. | No |

## RFC 1876: A Means for Expressing Location Information in the Domain Name System

| Section | Code location | Explanation | Documented |
|---|---|---|---|
| §2 RDATA Format | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone/rdata/advanced.rs:40-76`, `crates/pharos-proto/src/rdata.rs:643-665,963-965` | **Partial**: Zone-file LOC encoding emits the 16-octet format and zero VERSION, but wire parsing treats LOC as opaque. It accepts any payload length and never checks the required VERSION field. | No |
| §2 RDATA Format | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone/rdata/advanced.rs:301` | **Violation**: LONGITUDE minutes and seconds are truncated with `as u32`, so fractions below one thousandth of a second round toward the prime meridian; the spec requires rounding away from it. Latitude shares this path, but the spec states no rounding rule for it. No ADR or plugin README records the difference. | No |
| §3 Master File Format | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone/rdata/advanced.rs:49-68` | **Violation**: Altitude conversion does not enforce the specified `[-100000.00 .. 42849672.95]` meter range in 0.01-meter steps; out-of-range values can wrap. The nearby citation names §2, not §3. | No |
| §3 Master File Format | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone/rdata/advanced.rs:69-72` | **Violation**: The parser consumes at most three precision fields and silently ignores additional fields, although the master-file format ends at `vp`. | No |
| §3 Master File Format | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone/rdata/advanced.rs:268-316` | **Partial**: The coordinate parser accepts degrees alone or degrees, minutes, and seconds. It rejects the valid form with minutes but omitted seconds, allowed by `d [m [s]] hemisphere`. | Yes |

## RFC 1982: Serial Number Arithmetic

| Section | Code location | Explanation | Documented |
|---|---|---|---|
| §2 Serial Number Arithmetic | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/auto/walk.rs:48-53`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/file/mod.rs:142-144`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:110-112` | **Violation**: Initial zone loading passes serial zero as a sentinel, so a valid SOA serial of zero is rejected as “no change”; RFC 1982 gives zero no special significance. | No |
| §3.2 Comparison | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/k8s_external/transfer.rs:548-552,589-597`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/sign/output.rs:32-37`, `crates/pharos-proto/src/response.rs:100-106`, `crates/pharos-proto/tests/response.rs:101-105` | **Violation**: IXFR and RRSIG-time comparisons do not consistently use 32-bit half-space serial ordering; values near wraparound can be classified incorrectly. | No |
| §7 The DNS SOA serial number (SOA.expire increment window) | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/update.rs:184-195` | **Partial**: Each changed UPDATE increments by one, but the implementation does not limit cumulative increments within the SOA expire period. More than 2^31−1 changes in that period can leave secondaries misordering the zone serial. | No |
| §7 The DNS SOA serial number (serial ordering) | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/k8s_external/transfer.rs:548-552,589-597` | **Violation**: IXFR handling compares SOA serials with ordinary unsigned `>=`, so wraparound and half-space values can be misclassified as current. No plugin README or ADR documents this exception. | No |
| §7 The DNS SOA serial number (zero value) | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/file/mod.rs:142-144`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:110-112` | **Violation**: Initial file loading uses serial zero as a sentinel and rejects an SOA whose serial is zero as “no change.” Section 7 says zero is not special in serial arithmetic; no plugin README or ADR documents this exception. | No |

## RFC 1995: Incremental Zone Transfer in DNS

| Section | Code location | Explanation | Documented |
|---|---|---|---|
| §2 Brief Description of the Protocol | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/setup.rs:214-216`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/store.rs:27-31`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/store.rs:73-80`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/transfer.rs:11-16` | **Partial**: With `database`, updates are committed before the live Zone changes. Without it, updates remain in memory and the same live Zone serves transfers before durable storage; no documented exception. | No |
| §2 Brief Description of the Protocol | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/transfer.rs:26-30`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/kubernetes/transfer.rs:356-365`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/k8s_external/transfer.rs:548-552`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/k8s_external/transfer.rs:589-598` | **Violation**: Generic and Kubernetes transfers return one SOA only for an equal serial, not a newer client serial. External Kubernetes uses ordinary integer `>=`, which fails serial-number wraparound. | Yes |
| §3 Query Format | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/transfer/mod.rs:232-244` | **Partial**: The IXFR parser requires one SOA RDATA but does not verify that its owner and class match the requested zone and class, so a foreign-zone SOA is accepted as the client's version. | Yes |
| §6.3.1 Connection Reuse | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/secondary/update.rs:181-200`, `crates/pharos-server/src/transfer.rs:165-173` | **Violation**: The secondary checks the primary SOA on one TCP connection, closes it, then opens another for AXFR, contrary to RFC 9103's SHOULD to reuse an open connection. No listed exception is documented. | No |
| §6.3.4 The edns-tcp-keepalive EDNS(0) Option | `crates/pharos-server/src/outcome.rs:55-65`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/transfer.rs:57-73` | **Violation**: RFC 9103 requires every AXFR response to carry an OPT record when the request has one. Streamed TCP transfers return the Stream without OPT, and Zone transfer Messages are built without OPT. No plugin README or ADR documents this exception. | No |

## RFC 1996: A Mechanism for Prompt Notification of Zone Changes (DNS NOTIFY)

| Section | Code location | Explanation | Documented |
|---|---|---|---|
| §1.2 DNS NOTIFY transaction | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/secondary/mod.rs:162-192` | **Violation**: the handler accepts NOTIFY with any QTYPE and schedules a zone refresh; this specification limits notifications to SOA RR changes. | No |
| §2.1 Notify Set | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/transfer/mod.rs:54-76,114-157` | **Violation**: outgoing NOTIFY uses only explicitly configured `to` destinations; code does not implement the default NS RRset minus the SOA MNAME. | No |
| §3.2 NOTIFY message fields | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/secondary/mod.rs:162-190` | **Violation**: The receiver accepts NOTIFY messages without checking that undescribed header fields are zero. It processes accepted messages and copies selected request flags into the response instead of ignoring nonconforming messages. | No |
| §3.2 NOTIFY message fields | `xtask/src/query.rs:98-106,316-320` | **Partial**: `+opcode=notify` inherits RD=1 from the default recursion setting, though RFC 1996 requires undescribed fields to be zero. `+norec` can clear RD, but the parser does not enforce this for NOTIFY. | No |
| §3.3 NOTIFY request QR state | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/secondary/mod.rs:162-170` | **Violation**: The handler processes every NOTIFY opcode without requiring QR clear, so a NOTIFY response from a configured primary is treated as a new request. The nearby RFC 1996 §3.10 citation covers primary authorization, not QR state. | No |
| §3.3 NOTIFY response QR state | `crates/pharos-server/src/transfer.rs:71-83`, `crates/pharos-server/tests/transfer.rs:583-601` | **Violation**: The sender accepts any same-ID NOERROR message without requiring QR set, so a query can clear the retry attempt as though it were a response. The test explicitly preserves acceptance without QR or NOTIFY opcode. | No |
| §3.4 Transport protocol | `crates/pharos-server/src/transfer.rs:62-70` | **Partial**: NOTIFY sender always uses UDP and has no TCP path when TCP is necessary, such as when a firewall blocks UDP. | No |
| §3.6 Retry parameters | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/transfer/mod.rs:93-100`, `crates/pharos-server/src/transfer.rs:67` | **Partial**: Retry timing and count are not administrator-configurable; caller fixes timeout at two seconds and sender makes three total attempts. | Yes |
| §3.6 UDP retransmission and response matching | `crates/pharos-server/src/transfer.rs:67-91` | **Partial**: Connected UDP filters source IP and port, and sender checks the response ID, but it does not check response QNAME. Socket errors, including a surfaced ICMP port-unreachable error, lead to another attempt instead of stopping. | Yes |
| §3.7 NOTIFY counts | `crates/pharos-server/src/admission.rs:41-42` | **Violation**: §3.7 allows NOTIFY requests with ANCOUNT, AUCOUNT and ADCOUNT above zero, but admission answers any non-UPDATE message with ANCOUNT>1, NSCOUNT>1 or ARCOUNT>2 with FORMERR before the NOTIFY handler runs. | No |
| §3.9 Authority and Additional sections | `crates/pharos-server/src/admission.rs:41-42,45-47`, `crates/pharos-proto/src/message.rs:203-240` | **Violation**: §3.9 requires implementations to ignore the Authority and Additional sections but not the whole message. Admission refuses NOTIFY with NSCOUNT>1 or ARCOUNT>2, and the generic parser rejects a malformed Authority or Additional record or bad OPT, so the whole message gets FORMERR. | No |
| §3.10 NOTIFY from unknown master | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/secondary/mod.rs:162-171` | **Partial**: It drops requests from unknown masters. It does not write the required error to the operations log. | Yes |
| §3.11 Query the NOTIFY source | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/secondary/mod.rs:191-192`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/secondary/update.rs:178-231`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/secondary/update.rs:75-85` | **Violation**: The handler discards the sender address. The subsequent SOA query and transfer iterate configured masters instead of using the master that sent this NOTIFY. | No |
| §3.11 SOA NOTIFY processing | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/secondary/mod.rs:162-192`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/auto/mod.rs:107-125` | **Violation**: The handler queues work for every NOTIFY QTYPE, and periodic Auto scans notify all zones even when their SOA did not change. Both exceed RFC 1996's only defined event: an SOA RR change. | No |
| §3.12 NOTIMP completes transaction | `crates/pharos-server/src/transfer.rs:65-81` | **Violation**: The sender treats NOTIMP as an error and retries. RFC says a master should consider that transaction complete for the slave. | No |
| §4.4 Defer action on duplicate NOTIFY | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/secondary/mod.rs:191-192`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/secondary/update.rs:283-297` | **Violation**: each accepted NOTIFY increments a pending count, then starts an independent SOA check. Duplicate notifications are not keyed by `<QNAME,QCLASS,QTYPE>` or deferred until the first transaction completes. | No |
| §4.8 Master Receives a NOTIFY Response from Slave | `crates/pharos-server/src/transfer.rs:71-83`, `crates/pharos-server/tests/transfer.rs:583-601` | **Partial**: The sender accepts any same-ID `NOERROR` packet from the connected peer without requiring QR=1, NOTIFY opcode, or the matching SOA question, so a non-NOTIFY response can stop retries. The test explicitly preserves acceptance without QR or NOTIFY opcode. | No |

## RFC 2136: Dynamic Updates in the Domain Name System (DNS UPDATE)

| Section | Code location | Explanation | Documented |
|---|---|---|---|
| §1.1.1 Two RRs are considered equal | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/update.rs:67-83`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/mod.rs:228-288` | **Partial**: Matching compares owner, type, and parsed RDATA while ignoring TTL, but drops the received RDLENGTH when `expand_record` expands RDATA. Thus differently encoded records with different RDLENGTH can compare equal; the nearby citation also attributes case-insensitive name comparison to §1.1.1, while §1.1.2 supplies that rule. | No |
| §1.1.5 RR types that cannot be appended | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/setup.rs:63-147`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/update.rs:87-133`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/update.rs:67-83,143-150` | **Partial**: SOA and CNAME use their specified replacement rules, but WKS (type 11) is omitted from `known()` and rejected; if accepted, full-RDATA equality would also miss the required ADDRESS-and-PROTOCOL tuple match. | No |
| §1.4 Data and message signatures | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/store.rs:66-80`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dnssec/mod.rs:27-47` | **Partial**: With the DNSSEC plugin enabled, a secure UPDATE commits changed Zone data without zone-key signatures; DNSSEC signing happens only for later DNSSEC-requested responses. Same gap as §4.3. | No |
| §2.2 Message Header | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/mod.rs:292-295` | **Violation**: `reply` sets AA (`0x0400`) on every UPDATE response, although UPDATE uses only QR as a flag bit. | No |
| §2.2 Message Header | `crates/pharos-server/src/admission.rs:41-47,68-74` | **Violation**: Malformed UPDATEs get FORMERR responses with opcode bits cleared, but RFC 2136 requires copying the request opcode into the response. | No |
| §2.5 Changes to the CNAME Resource Record | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/setup.rs:41-59`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/sign/mod.rs:1-3` | **Partial**: RFC 4035 requires RRSIG and NSEC at CNAME owners in signed zones. Dynamic update rejects these record types, and Pharos signing is offline and file-based, so dynamic updates cannot maintain the required signed-zone data. | No |
| §3.1 Standard Policies — per-name access control | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/setup.rs:32-38,254-294` | **Partial**: Rules allow exact owners and `*` for all zone names, but provide no concise selector for a principal's own name or its subdomains, as recommended. | No |
| §3.1 Standard Policies — per-type access control | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/setup.rs:158-185,276-293` | **Partial**: Rules allow individual types and `*` for all types, but provide no concise selector for the recommended “user types” set. | No |
| §3.1.1 Process Zone Section | `crates/pharos-server/src/server.rs:537-542` | **Violation**: A missing handler or unmatched authority block returns REFUSED; RFC 2136 requires NOTAUTH for a zone outside the server's authority and NOTIMP when UPDATE is unimplemented. | No |
| §3.2.1 Prerequisites with CLASS ANY | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/update.rs:146-168` | **Partial**: Supported types get the required name/RRset existence result. Unsupported types return NOTIMP or FORMERR before lookup; malformed prerequisites of those types can return NOTIMP instead of the required FORMERR. | No |
| §3.2.2 Prerequisites with CLASS NONE | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/update.rs:146-168` | **Partial**: Supported types get the required name/RRset absence result. Unsupported types return NOTIMP or FORMERR before lookup; malformed prerequisites of those types can return NOTIMP instead of the required FORMERR. | No |
| §3.2.3 Prerequisites with CLASS ZCLASS | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/update.rs:146-178, 181-205` | **Partial**: Supported types are grouped by owner and type and compared as complete RRsets. Unsupported types return NOTIMP before RRset comparison, rather than the required NXRRSET when the prerequisite set does not match. | No |
| §3.2.4 Table of metavalues used in Prerequisite Section | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/update.rs:146-205` | **Partial**: The table's ANY, NONE, and ZCLASS meanings are implemented for supported types. Unsupported types are rejected before these metavalue tests. | No |
| §3.2.5 Pseudocode for Prerequisite Section Processing | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/update.rs:137-207` | **Partial**: Pseudocode behavior matches for supported types. The early unsupported-type return can bypass required FORMERR, NXDOMAIN, NXRRSET, YXDOMAIN, or YXRRSET results. | No |
| §3.4.1.2 Update Section Prescan | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/setup.rs:63-147`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/update.rs:19-21,92-94` | **Violation**: WKS is an assigned RR type, not an unrecognized type. `known()` omits WKS, so a valid WKS UPDATE is rejected with FORMERR. | No |
| §3.4.2.1 System failure and rollback | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/update.rs:91-93`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/store.rs:66-80`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/mod.rs:143-156` | **Partial**: Candidate changes stay private and reported processing or storage errors become SERVFAIL. Infallible allocations during candidate construction can abort on out-of-memory before the server returns SERVFAIL; the existing §3.4.2 citation covers ordered application, not this failure requirement. | No |
| §3.4.2.2 Update | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/setup.rs:63-147`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/update.rs:92-132`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/update.rs:143-150` | **Violation**: WKS is absent from `known()` and valid Zone-class WKS updates are rejected with FORMERR; the required ADDRESS-and-PROTOCOL duplicate replacement therefore never occurs. | No |
| §3.4.2.2 Update | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/update.rs:52-64`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/update.rs:115-120`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/setup.rs:41-60` | **Partial**: RFC 4035 §2.5 requires RRSIG and NSEC to coexist with CNAME, and permits KEY for secure updates. Zone loading rejects these types, while the CNAME conflict check treats any non-CNAME RR as a conflict; signed-zone CNAME updates are unsupported. | No |
| §3.4.2.6 Table Of Metavalues Used In Update Section — Add to an RRset | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/update.rs:92-94`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/setup.rs:63-147` | **Partial**: The `zone / rrset / rr` addition mapping works only for recognized types. WKS is rejected before `apply_updates`, so the table's add operation is not implemented for every defined RR type. | No |
| §3.4.2.7 Pseudocode For Update Section Processing | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/setup.rs:63-147`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/update.rs:87-131` | **Missing**: WKS is absent from the accepted RR type list, so updates reject it before applying the required ADDRESS-and-PROTOCOL duplicate matching and replacement rule. | No |
| §3.4.2.7 Pseudocode For Update Section Processing | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/setup.rs:41-60`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/update.rs:46-64`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/update.rs:115-121` | **Partial**: RFC 4035 §2.5 allows RRSIG and NSEC alongside CNAME in signed zones, and RFC 3007 §4.3 requires affected RRsets to be signed after updates. The plugin rejects DNSSEC record types and cannot load or maintain signed zones. | No |
| §3.5 Stability | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/setup.rs:17-20,215-219`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/store.rs:27-30,66-80,184-187` | **Partial**: With a database, the update is committed before publishing the new zone. Database configuration is optional, so the memory-only mode acknowledges updates that are not committed to nonvolatile storage. | No |
| §3.8 Response | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/mod.rs:292-310` | **Violation**: The response copies the Zone question but omits the other request sections and their counts; RFC 2136 allows copying all UPDATE sections or zeroing all counts and omitting the original UPDATE, and neither form is used. The inline citation wrongly claims §3.8 requires preserving the question. | No |
| §4.3 Non-explicit updates to SIGs | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/store.rs:66-80`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dnssec/mod.rs:42-46` | **Partial**: With the DNSSEC plugin enabled, UPDATE commits the changed Zone without signing its affected RRsets. DNSSEC signatures are added later to DNSSEC-requested responses, not at UPDATE completion. | No |
| §4.4 Effects on the zone | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/update.rs:184-196`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/store.rs:66-80` | **Partial**: The SOA serial is incremented and explicit SOA updates are accepted, but no new NXT records are generated and no zone-key signatures are created at UPDATE completion. | No |

## RFC 2163: Using the Internet DNS to Distribute MIXER Conformant Global Address Mapping (MCGAM)

| Section | Code location | Explanation | Documented |
|---|---|---|---|
| §4 Domain Name Compression | `crates/pharos-proto/src/rdata.rs:148-199,963-965`, `crates/pharos-proto/src/message.rs:178-186`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/loadbalance/mod.rs:490-519` | **Partial**: The receive path recognizes PX name pointers for relocation but does not decompress them; `Rdata::parse_in_message` rejects PX, so `with_question` cannot re-encode it. RFC 3597 §4 says receivers SHOULD decompress PX. | No |
| §4 PX resource record | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone.rs:444-462,898-909`, `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone/rdata/legacy.rs:51-57` | **Violation**: Zone parsing accepts explicit non-IN classes, then parses PX RDATA by type without checking class. RFC 2163 defines PX only for class IN. | No |

## RFC 2181: Clarifications to the DNS Specification

| Section | Code location | Explanation | Documented |
|---|---|---|---|
| §4.1 UDP Source Address Selection | `crates/pharos-server/src/lib.rs:877-930`, `crates/pharos-server/src/udp.rs:71-84,128-143,393-407`, `crates/pharos-server/src/udp_linux.rs:22-55,57-72` | **Partial**: UDP receive retains the remote peer, not the query's destination address, and replies use the listener socket's generic send operation. A wildcard multi-homed listener therefore cannot guarantee replies use the address the query targeted; no RFC 2181 §4.1 citation or documented justification found. | No |
| §5 Resource Record Sets | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:68-71,194-223,445-457` | **Partial**: Zone lookup indexes records by owner and type, not class, so same-owner/type records from different classes can be emitted together. DNSSEC grouping uses owner, class, and type, but does not fix the authoritative Zone path. | No |
| §5 Resource Record Sets | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:194-198,201-223,445-457`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/k8s_external/scratch.rs:8-16`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/k8s_external/mod.rs:229-275,386-431`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/kubernetes/scratch.rs:10-24`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/kubernetes/mod.rs:870-885,906-925,1178-1250`, `crates/pharos-plugins/tests/allocations/kubernetes_large_allocations.rs:64,181-187` | **Partial**: Zone loading retains duplicate records, while Kubernetes deduplication keys compare textual IPs and case-sensitive DNS names even when serialization produces equivalent DNS RRs. The IPv6 allocation fixture confirms two equivalent AAAA records are emitted. | No |
| §5.1 Sending RRs from an RRSet | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dnssec/mod.rs:28-31,99-125` | **Violation**: DNSKEY interception ignores the query class and always emits an IN RRset. A non-IN DNSKEY query can therefore receive records outside its requested class. | No |
| §5.2 TTLs of RRs in an RRSet | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:445-457, crates/pharos-plugins/src/dnssec/signing.rs:202-227, crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/mod.rs:648-650` | **Partial**: DNSSEC signing normalizes selected RRsets and cache rewrites response TTLs, but plain Zone answers serialize each record's TTL independently. A Zone RRset with differing TTLs can be sent unchanged. | No |
| §5.2 TTLs of RRs in an RRSet | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/proxy/mod.rs:551-567` | **Violation**: The forward proxy checks Message structure and question matching, not TTL consistency. It can relay a mixed-TTL RRset instead of treating it as an error. | No |
| §5.2 TTLs of RRs in an RRSet | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/mod.rs:595-618,625-650` | **Missing**: Cache accepts upstream RRsets without checking authority or TTL agreement, then caches them. It does not discard a malformed non-authoritative RRset and seek an authoritative source. | No |
| §5.2 TTLs of RRs in an RRSet | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dnssec/signing.rs:46-65,202-227, crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/mod.rs:595-618,648-650` | **Partial**: The signer uses each RRset's lowest TTL, but cache applies the minimum TTL from the whole Response to every record. It does not preserve the required per-RRset minimum when other RRsets have lower TTLs. | No |
| §5.2 TTLs of RRs in an RRSet | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/rewrite/mod.rs:1881-1894`, `crates/pharos-plugins/tests/functional/rewrite.rs:968-991,4801-4877` | **Violation**: TTL rewrite rules clamp each RR independently, so records in one RRset can retain different TTLs. Tests cover isolated records, not equal TTLs across an RRset. | No |
| §5.4.1 Ranking data | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/mod.rs:473-503`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/store.rs:102-118` | **Violation**: Cache replaces an entry for the same query without ranking its source or authority. A less trustworthy response can replace data from a more trustworthy source. | No |
| §5.5 Sending RRSets (reprise) | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/forward/mod.rs:395-397`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/store.rs:300-324`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/kubernetes/external.rs:69-91`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/k8s_external/mod.rs:873-904` | **Partial**: Forward and cache retain upstream sections without checking for an RRset repeated across sections, and `k8s_external` can serialize duplicate nameserver address records from matching EndpointSlices. | No |
| §6.1 Zone authority | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:153-190` | **Partial**: Zone loading requires an apex SOA but does not require apex NS; an SOA-only zone can still serve authoritative responses. No documented compatibility rationale found. | No |
| §6.2 DNSSEC issues | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:950-961` | **Violation**: RFC 4035 §3.1.4 updates this rule: a DO-bit referral from a signed parent without a DS RRset MUST include the parent-side NSEC and its RRSIG with the delegation NS RRset. This path writes DS only and has no NSEC fallback. | No |
| §7.3 The SOA.MNAME field | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/local/mod.rs:17-18,171-193` | **Violation**: The Local plugin emits `localhost.` as MNAME, including for the `localhost.` zone SOA, where MNAME equals the zone name and does not identify its primary server. | No |
| §8 Time to Live (TTL) | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/setup.rs:139-148`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/store.rs:179-187` | **Violation**: `serve_stale` defaults `stale_ttl` to zero, and the cache returns expired records with TTL 0 unless configured otherwise. RFC 8767 §4 requires stale records in a response to have TTL greater than zero. | No |
| §8 Time to Live (TTL) | `crates/pharos-proto/src/message.rs:134`, `crates/pharos-proto/src/builder.rs:251-256`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/setup.rs:49-64`, `crates/pharos-dnssec/src/keys.rs:273-283`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dnssec/mod.rs:112-117`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/rewrite/mod.rs:719-742,1881-1894` | **Partial**: RFC 8767 §4 recommends capping TTLs at 604800 seconds. Cache defaults cap cached TTLs below this, but direct forwarding, authoritative answers, DNSKEY answers, and rewrites can preserve larger TTLs, and cache caps can be raised. | No |
| §8 Time to Live (TTL) | `crates/pharos-proto/src/zone.rs:870-895` | **Violation**: TTL parsing uses wrapping arithmetic, so an out-of-range decimal such as `18446744073709551616` can wrap to zero instead of being rejected as outside the unsigned 32-bit range. | No |
| §9 The TC (truncated) header bit | `crates/pharos-proto/src/builder.rs:312-334`, `crates/pharos-server/src/outcome.rs:243-299` | **Violation**: The builder and response scrubber set TC whenever any record is omitted, including optional Additional data. RFC 2181 §9 says to omit an extra RRset without setting TC when only extra information does not fit. | No |
| §9 The TC (truncated) header bit | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/proxy/mod.rs:407-425` | **Deviation**: A truncated UDP upstream response is retried over TCP only with `prefer_udp`; otherwise Pharos returns it. `crates/pharos-plugins/src/forward/README.md` documents this CoreDNS-compatible behavior. | Yes |
| §9 The TC (truncated) header bit; RFC 4035 §3.1.1 update | `crates/pharos-proto/src/builder.rs:312-334`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:459-468,984-1021` | **Violation**: If an Additional RRset fits but its RRSIG does not, adding that signature fails and sets TC. RFC 4035 says the server may keep the RRset and drop its Additional-section RRSIGs, but MUST NOT set TC solely for those omitted signatures. | No |
| §10.1 CNAME resource records | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:145-229, 613-627` | **Violation**: Primary Zone loading accepts multiple CNAME records with different targets at one alias. Lookup then follows the first target while emitting the full CNAME RRset. | No |
| §10.1 CNAME resource records | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:145-187`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dynupdate/update.rs:52-64` | **Violation**: Primary Zone loading accepts CNAME alongside unrestricted data. Dynamic Zone construction instead rejects every non-CNAME RR at that owner, including the RRSIG, NSEC, and KEY coexistence allowed by RFC 4035 §2.5. | No |
| §10.1 CNAME resource records | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/autopath/mod.rs:334-357` | **Violation**: Rebuild inserts a CNAME at the original question name, then copies source answers without checking for an existing CNAME there. A response containing that owner can therefore emit duplicate or conflicting CNAMEs. | No |
| §10.2 PTR records | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:145-187, 796-809` | **Violation**: Zone loading does not check whether PTR RDATA names a CNAME owner, and lookup returns the PTR target unchanged. | No |
| §10.3 MX and NS records | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:145-187, 984-1021` | **Violation**: Zone loading accepts NS and MX targets that are aliases or lack address records. Lookup adds available in-Zone A/AAAA records, but does not validate either target constraint. | No |

## RFC 2230: Key Exchange Delegation Record for the DNS

| Section | Code location | Explanation | Documented |
|---|---|---|---|
| §3.1 KX RDATA format — A additional processing | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:985-991` | **Missing**: Additional processing handles MX, SVCB, HTTPS, SRV, and NS, but not KX; a KX answer therefore does not trigger A lookup for its exchanger. | No |
| §3.1 KX RDATA format — AAAA additional processing | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:985-991` | **Missing**: KX is absent from additional processing, so the server does not trigger AAAA lookup for its exchanger, despite supporting IPv6. | No |
| §4 Security Considerations — signed KX records | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:144-190`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/dnssec/signing.rs:67-109` | **Partial**: The DNSSEC plugin signs response RRsets when configured, but zone loading and serving permit unsigned KX records. RFC 2230 requires every KX record to be signed. | No |

## RFC 2308: Negative Caching of DNS Queries (DNS NCACHE)

| Section | Code location | Explanation | Documented |
|---|---|---|---|
| §2.1 Name Error | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:812-853,889-910` | **Violation**: On a CNAME-to-NXDOMAIN lookup with DO set, the zone copies external answers but not its authority records, then emits its own SOA; it adds NSEC proofs only when the original QNAME is absent. The final denied name therefore lacks the NSEC and RRSIG proof required by RFC 4035 §3.1.3.2. | No |
| §2.1 Name Error | `crates/pharos-proto/src/response.rs:70-76`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/mod.rs:348-355,573-585` | **Violation**: A valid NXDOMAIN response with CNAME answers is classified as `OtherError`, so cache admission rejects it instead of treating its final-cycle RCODE as NXDOMAIN. RFC 6604 §3 requires the response RCODE to reflect that final query cycle. | No |
| §2.1 Name Error | `crates/pharos-plugins/tests/allocations/kubernetes_services.rs:189-190`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/kubernetes/README.md:29` | **Deviation**: A SOA query for a nonexistent name returns NOERROR instead of NXDOMAIN, matching documented CoreDNS behavior allowed by ADR 0002. | Yes |
| §2.2 No Data | `crates/pharos-proto/src/response.rs:70-96`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/cache/mod.rs:573-580` | **Violation**: NOERROR with no relevant answer and no NS records is NODATA even when the authority section has no SOA. The classifier returns Positive without an SOA, including for a CNAME-only response, so cache handling misses the RFC 2308 §2.2 NODATA case. | No |
| §2.2 No Data | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/kubernetes/mod.rs:744-753`, `crates/pharos-plugins/tests/allocations/kubernetes_services.rs:187-188`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/kubernetes/README.md:28` | **Deviation**: An existing reverse name with a PTR RRset returns NXDOMAIN for an A query instead of NODATA, matching documented CoreDNS behavior allowed by ADR 0002. | Yes |
| §2.2 No Data | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/kubernetes/mod.rs:744-753,967-1007`, `crates/pharos-plugins/tests/allocations/kubernetes_services.rs:552-554`, `crates/pharos-plugins/src/kubernetes/README.md:27` | **Deviation**: Existing zone, service, and namespace names without TXT data return NXDOMAIN instead of NODATA, matching documented CoreDNS behavior allowed by ADR 0002. | Yes |
| §2.2 No Data | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/kubernetes/mod.rs:744-753,967-1007`, `crates/pharos-plugins/tests/allocations/kubernetes_services.rs:555` | **Violation**: `ns.dns.cluster.local.` has generated A/AAAA data, so a TXT query is NODATA, not NXDOMAIN; the compatibility notes do not document this case. | No |
| §2.2 No Data | `crates/pharos-plugins/src/zone/mod.rs:541-544,676-687`, `crates/pharos-plugins/tests/functional/file_coredns.rs:1007-1015` | **Violation**: An apex-only zone returns NXDOMAIN for an existing apex without the requested type instead of NODATA.
